Optimization algorithms:

1. **Active set / subgradient projected gradient** with line search and annealing. Since objective scale-invariant, maintain simplex.
   - At each step compute c, max_lag (maybe multiple active high values).
   - Gradient g_j = 2 ∑_{k in active} p_{k-j} (valid). Use softmax over top lags for smoothness.
   - Update p = project_simplex(p - lr * g + noise).
   - Line search over lr trying to reduce score or smoothed surrogate.
   - Need escape local minima: restarts, mixture with parent, random perturbations, pair moves.

2. **LP best-response**: For current p, M=max c (maybe with small margin ε or current best). Solve max ∑q s.t. c_pq = p * q ≤ M (elementwise), q≥0. This is a linear program with variables n and constraints 2n-1. A is Toeplitz/convolution matrix. Could solve with scipy linprog highs. n=700: A size 1399x700 ~979k entries, okay. Need build efficiently. Alternatively use `sparse` csr. Constraint A @ q ≤ M. Objective -ones. LP solution often extreme with many zeros. Then blend with p. Iterate several times. 3. **Coordinate descent / mass transfer**: Since objective max c, reducing max lag k by moving mass from positions contributing to c_k to positions not contributing? Pseudo:
```
def local_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, allow_lp=False, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = fp_score(p)
    cur_score = best_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    end = time.monotonic()+time_limit
    iter=0; since=0
    if allow_lp: do LP
    temperature_factor = 0.5
    while time < end:
       c = conv_full(p)
       cmax = max(c)
       # maybe use best c? 
       # Build active set: top K lags with values >= cmax - delta. delta = max(1e-12, (cmax - min(c))*T)
       # For gradient, use weights top K maybe softmax over top sorted.
       # Try line search steps.
       # gradient methods:
       # 1. active uniform top K (K=1,3,5,10)
       # 2. smooth softmax all with T factor.
       # 3. random single lag near max.
       # We can robustly try several candidate updates per iteration: 
       #   - projected p - lr*grad_active
       #   - pair move
       #   - random perturb
       # accept if score < cur or < best.
```

def active_indices(c, K=5, rel_delta=1e-8, rng=None, random_prob=0):

LP function:
```
def lp_best_response(p, max_m=None, bound_hi=10.0):
    p=normalize(p); n=p.size
    c=conv_full(p); M=max(c)
    if max_m is not None: M=max(M*1.0000001, max_m)
    build A sparse
    c_obj=-ones(n)
    res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,bound_hi), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
    q=res.x
    q=normalize(q)
    return q, M
```
But we want blend that improves p*p, not just q. We can test t in [0,1]. If LP q has better score, update.

Potential LP infeasibility due to M below p*p numeric. Use M = max(c)*1.000001 + 1e-12. Also because constraints p*q≤M. Feasible q=0, so feasible. Objective max sum; no issue. Bounds ensure bounded.

`convolution(p)`:
```
def conv_full(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n <= 1500: # maybe np.convolve fast enough and exact enough
        return np.convolve(p,p)
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=fft(p,L)
    c=np.real(ifft(F*F))
    return c[:2*n-1].astype(float, copy=False)
```
For large n, FFT has small roundoff; max may be slightly over/under. For final using actual evaluate_sequence, it uses exact np.convolve, no FFT. For internal search, error small. To be safe for n≤2000 use np.convolve? 2800 np.convolve ~7.8M complex? Actually real convolution O(n^2) 7.8M, maybe 1ms. 1e5 evals maybe 100s? Use FFT for >1800.

Local optimizer pseudo detailed:
```
def local_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, label='', allow_lp=False, max_lp=3):
    p=normalize(p0)
    n=p.size
    c=conv_full(p); cmax=max(c); p_score=2*n*cmax (since sum1)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=p_score
    cur_score=p_score
    prev_p=p.copy()
    end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    since=0; iter=0; lp_count=0
    if allow_lp: do up to min(max_lp, time) 
    base_K= max(3, n//100) # small
    for iter in ...:
       # if time for LP
       if allow_lp and (lp_count<max_lp or (iter%dვე ==...)) and time enough:
          do LP, if updated reset since
       # choose active lags
       sort_idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       # threshold: values within 5% of spread from max to 90th percentile
       p90 = c[sort_idx[min(n/10,)]] maybe
       thresh = cmax - max(1e-12, 0.05*(cmax - p90))
       active = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
       if active.size==0: active=sort_idx[:1]
       if active.size>MAX_ACTIVE (e.g., 80): choose top MAX_ACTIVE or random among those near
       g = active_gradient(p, active)
       # step lrs
       gmax=max(g); pavg=1/n
       base=np.sqrt(pavg/gmax) maybe? If step in proj simplex, lr* g ~p. base = pavg/gmax.
       candidates=[]
       for f in [1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
           v=p - base*f*g
           if iter>300: v +=0.25*(p-prev_p)
           pnew=project_simplex(v)
           evaluate
       # pair moves using g
       if not accepted:
          hi_idx = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:3]
          lo_idx = np.argsort(g)[:3]
          for hi in hi_idx:
            for lo in lo_idx:
              for frac in (0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01):
                 if p[hi]<=0: continue
                 delta=frac*p[hi]
                 pnew=p.copy(); pnew[hi]-=delta; pnew[lo]+=delta; pnew=project_simplex(pnew)
                 evaluate
       # random if none
       if not accepted:
          for sigma in (0.5/n,0.1/n,0.02/n,0.004/n):
             pnew=project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
             evaluate with anneal maybe
       # stuck mix with best
       if since > 500:
          p = project_simplex(0.5*best_p +0.5*p + rng.normal(0,1/n,n)) ; update cur_score
          since=0
       if accepted update c/cmax? If trial score computed via conv, we can use its c.
    return best_p, best_score
```

Let's implement `polish_lp(p, rng, time_limit, max_lp_iters=20)`:
```
p=normalize(p); best_p=p; best_score=fast_score(p)
end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
it=0
line_search=(1.0,0.8,0.65,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02)
while time<end and it<max_lp_iters:
   it+=1
   q=lp_best_response(p)
   if q is None: break
   improved=False
   for t in line_search:
      cand=normalize((1-t)*p + t*q)
      sc=fast_score(cand)
      if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
         best_score=sc; best_p=cand.copy(); p=cand.copy(); improved=True; break
      if sc < fast_score(p): # update current maybe
         p=cand.copy(); improved=True
   if not improved: break
   if best_score < 1.5005: break
return best_p,best_score
```
We need avoid re-evaluating p each line. Store p_score. After p updated during t loop, if break due best, okay. If a lower t improves p_score but not best? Could update p. But we only need best. We can line search monotonic? Since score along blend may nonconvex. Test several t and choose best candidate (not necessarily accept first). This costs len(t)*eval=10. For each LP, okay.
```
best_cand=None; best_t_sc=p_score

Let's detail `active_gradient`:
```
def active_gradient(p, idx):
    n=p.size
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    idx=np.asarray(idx,int)
    for k in idx:
        k=int(k)
        lo = k - (n-1)
        if lo < 0: lo=0
        hi = k
        if hi > n-1: hi=n-1
        if hi >= lo:
            l = np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=int)
            j = k - l
            g[j] += p[l]
    return 2.0*g
```
If idx contains duplicates? no. If idx large, vectorizing might be better. For each active, creates arange; okay. For K up to 60 and n700, 42k per iter, fine.

`smooth_gradient` maybe useful if active stuck. Could implement but not necessary. Maybe use for some updates. If we want, define:
```
def smooth_gradient(p, w):
   n=p.size; if n<=LP? use direct weighted if len(w small? But w full.
   L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   Fp=fft(p,L); Fw=fft(w[::-1],L)
   h=np.real(ifft(Fp*Fw))
   G=h[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
   return 2*G
```
Compute weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, temp_scale):
    cmax=max(c)
    spread = cmax - np.percentile(c, 90) maybe
    T=max(1e-12, spread*temp_scale)
    e=np.exp((c-cmax)/T); w=e/sum
    return w
```

Local optimizer code detail:
```
def local_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, label='', allow_smoother=False):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n==0: return p, inf
    c = conv_full(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    p_score = 2.0*n*cmax  # since sum1
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = p_score; best_c = cmax
    cur_score = p_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit)
    iter_no=0; since=0
    base_K = max(5, n//50)
    anneal_temp = 0.0015
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       iter_no+=1
       anneal_temp = max(0.0, anneal_temp*0.999 - 1e-7)
       order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       cmax = float(c[order[0]])
       # if cmax mismatch p_score? p normalized sum1, score=2n cmax. Could update cur_score=c? If p unchanged, p_score should be 2n cmax.
       p_score = 2.0*n*cmax
       K = base_K + min(64, since//20)
       K = min(K, order.size)
       active = order[:K]
       g = active_gradient(p, active)
       gabs=float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
       accepted=False
       if gabs > 1e-14:
          base = (1.0/n)/gabs
          base = float(np.clip(base, 1e-5, 10.0))
          factors = (1.0,0.7,0.45,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015)
          momentum = 0.0 if iter_no<200 else 0.25
          for f in factors:
             v = p - base*f*g
             if momentum>0: v = v + momentum*(p-prev_p)
             pnew = project_simplex(v)
             cnew = conv_full(pnew); cmax_new = float(np.max(cnew)); scnew=2.0*n*cmax_new
             # acceptance
             if np.isfinite(scnew):
                if scnew < cur_score - 1e-14 or (anneal_temp>0 and scnew < cur_score + anneal_temp and rng.random()<math.exp(-(scnew-cur_score)/anneal_temp)):
                    accept
       # smooth every 80
       if not accepted and allow_smoother and iter_no % 80 == 40:
          # weights over order top 2K maybe uniform over top 2K? Use smooth_gradient with top 2K one-hot? That's active with larger K. Instead compute softmax over top 2K values:
          topv = c[order[:min(2*K, order.size)]]
          T = max(1e-12, (topv[0]-topv[-1])+1e-15)
          e=np.exp((topv-topv[0])/T)
          wfull = np.zeros(2*n-1); wfull[order[:topv.size]] = e/e.sum()
          g2 = smooth_gradient(p, wfull)
          ... line search as above
       # pair moves if no accepted
       if not accepted:
          hi = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:4]
          lo = np.argsort(g)[:4]
          for hi_i in hi:
             if p[hi_i]<=0: continue
             for lo_i in lo:
                 if lo_i==hi_i: continue
                 for frac in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01):
                    delta=frac*p[hi_i]
                    if delta<=0: continue
                    pnew=p.copy(); pnew[hi_i]-=delta; pnew[lo_i]+=delta
                    if pnew[hi_i] < -1e-15: ...
                    pnew=project_simplex(pnew)
                    eval, accept (no anneal maybe if sc < cur)
       # random if no accepted
       if not accepted:
          sigmas=(0.5/n,0.1/n,0.02/n,0.004/n,0.0008/n)
          for sigma in sigmas:
             pnew=project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
             eval; accept with anneal if sc < cur+temp
       # if stuck mix with best
       if not accepted:
          since+=1
          if since > 120:
             mix=0.7 if since<400 else 0.5
             p = project_simplex(mix*best_p + (1-mix)*p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2/n, n))
             c=conv_full(p); cmax=...; cur_score=...
             prev_p=p.copy(); since=0
          if since > 800:
             p = best_p.copy(); c=conv_full(p); cmax=...; cur_score=best_score; since=0
       else:
          update current; prev_p old; since=0 if improved best? else since maybe 0? We can reset since when any accepted (even anneal worsen?) Set since=0.
       # periodically print
       if iter_no % 1000 ==0: print(label + ...)
    return best_p, best_score
```
Acceptance code repeated; we can make small local variables. Since no closure, can write helper top-level `accept_trial(scnew, cur_score, best_score, anneal_temp, rng)`? It returns bool. But also need random. Use top-level:
```
def should_accept(scnew, cur_score, anneal_temp, rng):
    if not np.isfinite(scnew): return False
    if scnew < cur_score - 1e-14: return True
    if anneal_temp > 0 and scnew <= cur_score + anneal_temp:
       # avoid accepting equal/worse with prob if not worse
       if scnew <= cur_score: return True
       ratio = -(scnew-cur_score)/anneal_temp
       if ratio > 50: return False
       return rng.random() < math.exp(ratio)
    return False
```

Implement `_lp_structure(n)`:
Top-level global dict `_LP_CACHE = {}`.
```
def get_lp_structure(n):
   if n in _LP_CACHE: return _LP_CACHE[n]
   rows=[]; cols=[]; larr=[]
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(k,n-1)
      if hi>=lo:
         l=np.arange(lo,hi+1,dtype=np.int64)
         j=k-l
         rows.extend(l.tolist?) but building list of ints length n^2. Better preallocate? For n=700, 490k lists okay. Use np.concatenate arrays in loop to reduce overhead.
   rows=np.vstack? Simpler: accumulate lists of Python ints maybe memory. Use numpy arrays:
      rows_parts.append(np.full(l.size,k,dtype=np.int32))
      cols_parts.append(j.astype(np.int32))
      l_parts.append(l.astype(np.int32))
   rows=np.concatenate(rows_parts); cols=np.concatenate(cols_parts); l=np.concatenate(l_parts)
   _LP_CACHE[n]=(rows,cols,l)
   return
```

Let's define `softmax_weights_top(c, order, K, T_factor)`:
```
top = c[order[:K]]
T = max(1e-15, (top[0]-top[-1]) * T_factor + 1e-15)
e = np.exp((top-top[0])/T)
w = np.zeros(c.size)
w[order[:K]] = e
w /= w.sum()
return w
```

def next_fast_len(n):
    n=int(max(1,n)); m=1
    while m<n: m <<=1
    return m

`project_simplex`: handle n=1. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.nan_to_num(...)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = (u * idx) > (cssum - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    k=int(np.count_nonzero(cond)-1)
    theta=(cssum[k]-1.0)/(k+1)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return out/s
```
Works.

```

def optimize_candidate_poly(p0, rng, time_limit, allow_lp, label=''):
    p=normalize(p0); n=p.size
    sc=fast_score(p)
    end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    # If allow_lp, do split: lp 25-30%, local, lp, local.
    if allow_lp and _HAVE_LP and n <= LP_MAX:
        if time_limit > 500:
            lp1 = min(240.0, time_limit*0.25)
            loc1 = min(480.0, time_limit*0.4)

               p,sc=local_optimize(p,rng,loc1,label+'L1:')
               p,sc=polish_lp(p,rng,lp2,label+'LP2:')
               p,sc=local_optimize(p,rng,loc2,label+'L2:')

```
def should_accept_score(cand, cur, temp, rng):
   if not np.isfinite(cand): return False
   if cand < cur - 1e-13: return True
   if temp > 0.0 and cand <= cur + temp:
       x=(cand-cur)/temp
       if x > 50: return False
       if x <= 0: return True # maybe accept small equal/worse? For worse only if x>0
       return rng.random() < math.exp(-x)
   return False
```

Now, `get_lp_structure` uses global _LP_CACHE.

```
def get_lp_structure(n):
    key=int(n)
    if key in _LP_CACHE:
       return _LP_CACHE[key]
    rows_parts=[]; cols_parts=[]; l_parts=[]
    for k in range(2*key-1):
       lo = k - (key - 1)
       if lo < 0: lo=0
       hi = k
       if hi > key-1: hi=key-1
       if hi >= lo:
          l = np.arange(lo, hi+1, dtype=np.int64)
          j = k - l
          rows_parts.append(np.full(l.size, k, dtype=np.int64))
          cols_parts.append(j.astype(np.int64, copy=False))
          l_parts.append(l)
    rows=np.concatenate(rows_parts) if rows_parts else np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
    cols=np.concatenate(cols_parts) if cols_parts else np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
    larr=np.concatenate(l_parts) if l_parts else np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
    _LP_CACHE[key]=(rows,cols,larr)
    return _LP_CACHE[key]
```
For n=700, rows_parts 1399 arrays, concatenate. Fine.

The LP direction might still be good? Risk. We'll require success to avoid bad. But if HiGHS returns status 1 (maxiter) with near solution, ignoring may lose. if q is None: return None

Now, `local_optimize` with smooth gradient. Need `smooth_gradient` if n maybe 1, L etc. Implement safely.
```
def smooth_gradient(p, w):
   n=int(p.size)
   if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
   L=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   Fp=fft(p,L)
   Fw=fft(w[::-1],L)
   h=np.real(ifft(Fp*Fw))
   G=h[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
   if G.size != n:
      tmp=np.zeros(n); m=min(n,G.size); tmp[:m]=G[:m]; G=tmp
   return 2.0*np.maximum(G,0.0)
```
Note gradient should be nonnegative if w,p nonnegative; FFT roundoff may negative, clip.

`softmax_top_weights(c, order, K, t_factor=0.5)`:
```
if K<=0: return None
K=min(K, c.size)
idx=order[:K]
vals=c[idx]
if vals.size==0: return None
spread=float(vals[0]-vals[-1])
if spread < 1e-15:
   w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=1.0/vals.size; return w
T=spread*t_factor
if T<=0: T=spread
e=np.exp((vals-vals[0])/T)
e=e/e.sum()
w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=e; return w
```
Need ensure `w` sum finite. Good.

```
def local_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0: return p, np.inf
    c = conv_full(p)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(n, c)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = cur_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score): cur_score = 1e100; best_score=1e100
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    iter_no=0; since_any=0; since_best=0
    base_K = max(5, n//50)
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       iter_no +=1
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
          c=conv_full(p)
       order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       cmax = float(c[order[0]])
       cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)  # p normalized
       # if p not normalized? ensure by normalize? Could skip.
       accepted=False; improved_best=False
       g=None
       # active gradient
       K = base_K + min(64, since_any // 10)
       K = min(K, order.size)
       g = active_gradient(p, order[:K])
       gabs = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
       if gabs > 1e-14:
          base = (1.0/n)/gabs
          base = float(np.clip(base, 1e-5, 10.0))
          trial_p=None; trial_score=np.inf; trial_c=None
          factors=(1.5,1.0,0.7,0.45,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03)
          momentum=0.0
          if iter_no > 300: momentum=0.25
          for f in factors:
             v = p - base * f * g
             if momentum > 0.0:
                v = v + momentum * (p - prev_p)
             pnew = project_simplex(v)
             cnew = conv_full(pnew)
             scnew = score_from_conv(n, cnew)
             if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < trial_score:
                trial_score=scnew; trial_p=pnew; trial_c=cnew
          if trial_p is not None:
             temp = 0.0
             if since_any > 150:
                temp = 0.001 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(since_any)/800.0)
             if should_accept_score(trial_score, cur_score, temp, rng):
                prev_p = p.copy()
                p = trial_p; c = trial_c; cur_score = trial_score
                accepted = True
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                   best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy(); since_best=0; improved_best=True
       if not accepted and (iter_no % 80 == 40 or (since_any > 100 and iter_no % 20 == 10)):
          w = softmax_top_weights(c, order, 2*base_K, 0.5)
          if w is not None:
             g2 = smooth_gradient(p, w)
             gabs2=float(np.max(g2)) if g2.size else 0.0
             if gabs2 > 1e-14:
                base2=(1.0/n)/gabs2
                base2=float(np.clip(base2,1e-5,10.0))
                trial... factors (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.18,0.09,0.04)
                temp...
                accept
       if not accepted and since_any > 30:
          # pair moves
          ...
       if not accepted and since_any > 50:
          # random
          ...
       if accepted:
          since_any=0
       else:
          since_any += 1
       if improved_best:
          since_best=0
       else:
          since_best += 1
       if not accepted and since_any > 400:
          mix = 0.7 if since_best < 600 else 0.4
          p = project_simplex(mix*best_p + (1.0-mix)*p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3/n, n))
          c = conv_full(p); cur_score=score_from_conv(n,c); prev_p=p.copy(); since_any=0
       elif since_best > 1200:
          p = best_p.copy(); c=conv_full(p); cur_score=best_score; prev_p=p.copy(); since_any=0; since_best=0
       if iter_no % 2000 == 0:
          print(label + " iter=%d best=%.10f cur=%.10f" % (iter_no, best_score, cur_score), flush=True)
       if best_score < 1.5 and since_best > 5000: # maybe break? no
          # could break if time not issue; leave
    return best_p, best_score
```

Now, **optimize_candidate_poly**: It needs access to `local_optimize`, `polish_lp`. We'll define after them.

```
def optimize_candidate_poly(p0, rng, time_limit, allow_lp, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    sc = fast_score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return p, sc
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    n = int(p.size)
    use_lp = bool(allow_lp) and _HAVE_LP and n <= LP_MAX
    if use_lp:
       if time_limit > 500:
          lp1 = min(240.0, max(30.0, time_limit * 0.22))
          loc1 = min(600.0, max(60.0, time_limit * 0.42))
          lp2 = min(150.0, max(20.0, time_limit * 0.15))
          loc2 = max(0.0, time_limit - lp1 - loc1 - lp2 - 20.0)
          # use actual remaining scheduling
          if time.monotonic() < end - 30:
             p,sc = polish_lp(p,rng, min(lp1, end-time.monotonic()-30), max_iters=6, label=label+"LP1 ")
          if time.monotonic() < end - 30:
             p,sc = local_optimize(p,rng, min(loc1, end-time.monotonic()-30), label=label+"LOC1 ")
          if time.monotonic() < end - 30 and n <= LP_MAX:
             p,sc = polish_lp(p,rng, min(lp2, end-time.monotonic()-30), max_iters=5, label=label+"LP2 ")
          if time.monotonic() < end - 10:
             p,sc = local_optimize(p,rng, min(loc2, end-time.monotonic()-10), label=label+"LOC2 ")
       else:
          p,sc=local_optimize(p,rng,time_limit,label)
    else:
       p,sc=local_optimize(p,rng,time_limit,label)
    if use_lp and time.monotonic() < end - 30:
       rem = end - time.monotonic() - 10
       if rem > 20:
          p,sc = polish_lp(p,rng,min(60.0,rem),max_iters=3,label=label+"LPf ")
    return p, sc
```
If time_limit large 1400, lp1=240, loc1=600, lp2=150, loc2=400 (minus 20). total1390. Good. If time left less, adapt.

```
def odd_poly_random_hill(n, rng, budget_s):
    global _OP_N
    _OP_N=n
    D=6
    theta=np.zeros(D)
    best_p=odd_poly_from_theta(theta,n); best_score=fast_score(best_p)
    end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    step=1.0
    while time < end:
       j=rng.integers(0,D); theta2=theta.copy(); theta2[j]+=rng.normal(0, step); theta2[j]=clip
       p=odd_poly_from_theta(theta2,n); sc=fast_score(p)
       if sc<best_score: theta=theta2; best_p=p; best_score=sc; step=max(0.1, step*1.05)
       else: step=max(0.05, step*0.95)
    return best_p,best_score
```
But this is local top-level? It doesn't use closures. Good. Use if no DE or as extra? Could use always if DE fails.

def search_odd_poly(n, rng, budget_s):
   if budget_s < 10: return None, inf
   best_p=None; best_score=inf
   if _HAVE_DE:
      global _OP_N; _OP_N=n
      D=min(8, max(4, n//100))
      bounds=[(-2.0,2.0)]*D
      try:
        res=differential_evolution(... maxiter=30, popsize=10, polish=False)
        theta=res.x; p=odd_poly_from_theta(theta,n); sc=fast_score(p); update
      except Exception: pass
   if _HAVE_DE false or best_score>some:
      p,s=odd_poly_random_hill(n,rng,max(10,budget_s*0.5))
   return best_p,best_score

def add_candidate(cands, p, label):
   if p is None: return
   arr=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   if arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0); arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12: return
   arr=arr/s
   sc=fast_score(arr)
   if np.isfinite(sc): cands.append((sc, arr.copy(), label))
```

def prune_candidates(cands, max_keep):
   if len(cands) <= max_keep: return cands
   cands.sort(key=cand_score_key)
   del cands[max_keep:]
   return cands
```

Implement `smooth_sequence(p, sigma)` top-level.
```
def smooth_sequence(p, sigma):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=p.size
   if n<5: return p
   h=3.0*sigma
   r=max(1,int(h))
   x=np.arange(-r,r+1)
   k=np.exp(-0.5*(x/sigma)**2)
   k=k/k.sum()
   q=np.convolve(p,k,'same')
   return normalize(q)
```
Add candidate from parent700 and overall with sigma 1.5,2.5,4.0. Could improve. In propose, after main optimization, if time, create smoothed versions and fast score; if better maybe local quick.

```

if temp>0 and cand > cur + 1e-13 and cand <= cur + temp: ...
return False
```
This ignores equal. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses:
  * exact convolution values for small/medium lengths,
  * projected gradient / line-search on the probability simplex with active-set
    convex subgradients of max(convolution),
  * a smoothed-max gradient fallback,
  * simple pair/random moves for escaping degenerate flat points,
  * an LP best-response iteration (fixed current p, maximize sum(q) subject to
    p*q <= max(p*p)) as a strong global-polish heuristic,
  * low-dimensional global shape explorations (power/exponential/tanh/odd-polynomial).
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

    def next_fast_len(n):
        n = int(max(1, n))
        m = 1
        while m < n:
            m <<= 1
        return m

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LINPROG = False

try:
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    _HAVE_CSR = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_CSR = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    _HAVE_DE = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_DE = False

_HAVE_LP = _HAVE_LINPROG and _HAVE_CSR
LP_MAX = 750
_LP_CACHE = {}
_OP_N = 0


def cand_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def job_score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def make_uniform(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return make_uniform(p.size)
    return p / s


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u * idx) > (cssum - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(np.count_nonzero(cond) - 1)
    theta = (cssum[k] - 1.0) / float(k + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def conv_full(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 2000:
        return np.convolve(p, p)
    L = int(next_fast_len(max(1, 2 * n - 1)))
    F = fft(p, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(F * F))
    return c[:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def score_from_conv(n, c):
    if c is None or len(c) == 0:
        return float("inf")
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * int(n) * cmax)


def fast_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_full(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def resample_sequence(seq, m):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(seq.size)
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if n == 0:
        return make_uniform(m)
    if n == m:
        return normalize(seq)
    xs = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xt = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    q = np.interp(xt, xs, seq)
    return normalize(q)


def smooth_sequence(p, sigma):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 5:
        return normalize(p)
    sigma = float(max(0.5, sigma))
    r = int(max(1, 3.0 * sigma))
    x = np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    k = np.exp(-0.5 * (x / sigma) ** 2)
    k = k / k.sum()
    q = np.convolve(p, k, mode="same")
    return normalize(q)


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size < 2 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s


def make_parametric(n, rng, max_candidates=160):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    out = []
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    out.append(make_uniform(n))

    for a in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        p = np.power(x + 1e-6, a)
        out.append(normalize(p))
        p = np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-6, a)
        out.append(normalize(p))

    for s in (-8.0, -4.0, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        p = np.exp(s * (x - 0.5))
        out.append(normalize(p))

    for A in (0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.3, 1.6, 1.9):
        for B in (1.5, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0, 24.0, 48.0):
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                p = 1.0 + sign * A * np.tanh(B * (x - 0.5))
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
                out.append(normalize(p))

    for a in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for b in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            p = 1.0 + a * z + b * z ** 3
            p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
            out.append(normalize(p))

    for a in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
        for b in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
            for c in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
                p = 1.0 + a * z + b * z ** 3 + c * z ** 5
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
                out.append(normalize(p))

    if len(out) > max_candidates:
        rng_idx = rng.permutation(len(out))[:max_candidates]
        out = [out[i] for i in rng_idx]
    return out


def add_candidate(cands, p, label):
    if p is None:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return
    arr = arr / s
    sc = fast_score(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((float(sc), arr.copy(), label))


def prune_candidates(cands, max_keep):
    if len(cands) <= max_keep:
        return cands
    cands.sort(key=cand_score_key)
    del cands[max_keep:]
    return cands


def odd_poly_from_theta(theta, n):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    D = int(min(theta.size, 10))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    z = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    z2 = z * z
    power = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(D):
        if m > 0:
            power = power * z2
        g += theta[m] * power
    g = g * z
    f = 1.0 + g
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-8)
    return normalize(f)


def odd_poly_objective(theta):
    n = int(_OP_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100
    p = odd_poly_from_theta(theta, n)
    sc = fast_score(p)
    return float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else 1e100


def odd_poly_random_hill(n, rng, budget_s):
    global _OP_N
    n = int(max(16, n))
    _OP_N = n
    D = 6
    theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    best_p = odd_poly_from_theta(theta, n)
    best_score = fast_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return None, float("inf")
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.5, float(budget_s))
    step = 1.0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        j = int(rng.integers(0, D))
        theta2 = theta.copy()
        theta2[j] += float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
        theta2[j] = float(np.clip(theta2[j], -3.0, 3.0))
        p = odd_poly_from_theta(theta2, n)
        sc = fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            theta = theta2
            best_p = p
            best_score = sc
            step = float(min(2.5, step * 1.05))
        else:
            step = float(max(0.05, step * 0.95))
    return best_p, best_score


def search_odd_poly(n, rng, budget_s):
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s < 10.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    if _HAVE_DE:
        try:
            global _OP_N
            n = int(max(16, n))
            _OP_N = n
            D = int(min(8, max(4, n // 100)))
            bounds = [(-2.5, 2.5) for _ in range(D)]
            seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            res = differential_evolution(
                odd_poly_objective,
                bounds,
                maxiter=30,
                popsize=10,
                tol=0.2,
                seed=seed,
                polish=False,
                init="latinhypercube",
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                p = odd_poly_from_theta(res.x, n)
                sc = fast_score(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = p
        except Exception:
            best_p = None
            best_score = float("inf")
    if best_p is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        p, sc = odd_poly_random_hill(n, rng, max(5.0, budget_s * 0.5))
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_p = p
            best_score = sc
    return best_p, best_score


def should_accept_score(cand, cur, temp, rng):
    if not np.isfinite(cand):
        return False
    if cand < cur - 1e-13:
        return True
    if temp > 0.0 and cand > cur + 1e-13 and cand <= cur + temp:
        x = float((cand - cur) / temp)
        if x > 60.0:
            return False
        return float(rng.random()) < math.exp(-x)
    return False


def active_gradient(p, idx):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return g
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    for k in idx:
        k = int(k)
        lo = k - (n - 1)
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k
        if hi > n - 1:
            hi = n - 1
        if hi >= lo:
            l = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            j = k - l
            g[j] += p[l]
    return 2.0 * g


def smooth_gradient(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(next_fast_len(max(1, 3 * n - 2)))
    Fp = fft(p, L)
    Fw = fft(w[::-1], L)
    h = np.real(ifft(Fp * Fw))
    G = h[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]
    if G.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, int(G.size))
        tmp[:m] = G[:m]
        G = tmp
    return 2.0 * np.maximum(G, 0.0)


def softmax_top_weights(c, order, K, t_factor=0.5):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    order = np.asarray(order, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    K = int(min(K, c.size, order.size))
    if K <= 0:
        return None
    idx = order[:K]
    vals = c[idx]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        return None
    spread = float(vals[0] - vals[-1])
    if spread <= 1e-15:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(vals.size)
        return w
    T = max(1e-15, spread * float(t_factor))
    e = np.exp((vals - vals[0]) / T)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(vals.size)
        return w
    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = e / s
    return w


def get_lp_structure(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n in _LP_CACHE:
        return _LP_CACHE[n]
    rows_parts = []
    cols_parts = []
    l_parts = []
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        lo = k - (n - 1)
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k
        if hi > n - 1:
            hi = n - 1
        if hi >= lo:
            l = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            j = k - l
            rows_parts.append(np.full(l.size, k, dtype=np.int64))
            cols_parts.append(j.astype(np.int64, copy=False))
            l_parts.append(l)
    if rows_parts:
        rows = np.concatenate(rows_parts)
        cols = np.concatenate(cols_parts)
        larr = np.concatenate(l_parts)
    else:
        rows = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
        cols = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
        larr = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    _LP_CACHE[n] = (rows, cols, larr)
    return _LP_CACHE[n]


def lp_best_response(p):
    if not _HAVE_LP:
        return None
    p = normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or n > LP_MAX:
        return None
    c = conv_full(p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    M = M * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-15
    try:
        rows, cols, larr = get_lp_structure(n)
        data = p[larr].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=(0.0, 1.0),
            method="highs",
            options={"disp": False},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None:
        return None
    q = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if q is None:
        return None
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return None
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return q / s


def polish_lp(p0, rng, time_limit, max_iters=6, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    sc = fast_score(p)
    if not _HAVE_LP or n > LP_MAX or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return p, sc
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = sc
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    ts = (1.0, 1.15, 1.3, 0.85, 0.7, 0.55, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03)
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < end and it < int(max_iters):
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() > end - 8.0:
            break
        q = lp_best_response(p)
        if q is None:
            break
        trial_p = None
        trial_score = fast_score(p)
        for t in ts:
            cand = project_simplex((1.0 - t) * p + t * q)
            s2 = fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < trial_score:
                trial_score = s2
                trial_p = cand.copy()
        if trial_p is None:
            break
        p = trial_p
        if p < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(p)
            best_p = p.copy()
        print(label + "LP iter=%d best=%.10f" % (it, best_score), flush=True)
        if best_score < 1.5000:
            break
    return best_p, best_score


def local_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    c = conv_full(p)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(n, c)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = 1e100
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    iter_no = 0
    since_any = 0
    since_best = 0
    base_K = max(5, n // 50)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        iter_no += 1

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            c = conv_full(p)
        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        cmax = float(c[order[0]])
        if not np.isfinite(cmax):
            c = conv_full(p)
            order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
            cmax = float(c[order[0]])
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        accepted = False
        improved_best = False
        g = None

        K = base_K + min(64, since_any // 10)
        K = int(min(K, order.size))
        g = active_gradient(p, order[:K])
        gabs = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0

        if gabs > 1e-14:
            base = (1.0 / float(n)) / gabs
            base = float(np.clip(base, 1e-5, 10.0))
            factors = (1.5, 1.0, 0.7, 0.45, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03)
            momentum = 0.0 if iter_no < 300 else 0.25
            trial_p = None
            trial_score = float("inf")
            trial_c = None
            for f in factors:
                v = p - base * f * g
                if momentum > 0.0:
                    v = v + momentum * (p - prev_p)
                pnew = project_simplex(v)
                cnew = conv_full(pnew)
                scnew = score_from_conv(n, cnew)
                if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < trial_score:
                    trial_score = scnew
                    trial_p = pnew
                    trial_c = cnew
            if trial_p is not None:
                temp = 0.0
                if since_any > 150:
                    temp = 0.001 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(since_any) / 800.0)
                if should_accept_score(trial_score, cur_score, temp, rng):
                    prev_p = p.copy()
                    p = trial_p
                    c = trial_c
                    cur_score = float(trial_score)
                    accepted = True
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        improved_best = True

        if not accepted and (iter_no % 80 == 40 or (since_any > 100 and iter_no % 20 == 10)):
            w = softmax_top_weights(c, order, 2 * base_K, 0.5)
            if w is not None:
                g2 = smooth_gradient(p, w)
                gabs2 = float(np.max(g2)) if g2.size else 0.0
                if gabs2 > 1e-14:
                    base2 = (1.0 / float(n)) / gabs2
                    base2 = float(np.clip(base2, 1e-5, 10.0))
                    factors2 = (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.04)
                    trial_p = None
                    trial_score = float("inf")
                    trial_c = None
                    for f in factors2:
                        v = p - base2 * f * g2
                        pnew = project_simplex(v)
                        cnew = conv_full(pnew)
                        scnew = score_from_conv(n, cnew)
                        if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < trial_score:
                            trial_score = scnew
                            trial_p = pnew
                            trial_c = cnew
                    if trial_p is not None:
                        temp = 0.0
                        if since_any > 150:
                            temp = 0.001 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(since_any) / 800.0)
                        if should_accept_score(trial_score, cur_score, temp, rng):
                            prev_p = p.copy()
                            p = trial_p
                            c = trial_c
                            cur_score = float(trial_score)
                            accepted = True
                            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                                best_score = cur_score
                                best_p = p.copy()
                                improved_best = True

        if not accepted and since_any > 30 and g is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            gorder = np.argsort(g)
            hi_list = gorder[-3:][::-1]
            lo_list = gorder[:3]
            trial_p = None
            trial_score = float("inf")
            trial_c = None
            for hi in hi_list:
                hi = int(hi)
                if p[hi] <= 0.0:
                    continue
                for lo in lo_list:
                    lo = int(lo)
                    if lo == hi:
                        continue
                    for frac in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.02):
                        delta = float(frac) * float(p[hi])
                        if delta <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        p2 = p.copy()
                        p2[hi] -= delta
                        p2[lo] += delta
                        c2 = conv_full(p2)
                        sc2 = score_from_conv(n, c2)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < trial_score:
                            trial_score = sc2
                            trial_p = p2
                            trial_c = c2
            if trial_p is not None:
                temp = 0.0
                if since_any > 200:
                    temp = 0.0008 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(since_any) / 1000.0)
                if should_accept_score(trial_score, cur_score, temp, rng):
                    prev_p = p.copy()
                    p = trial_p
                    c = trial_c
                    cur_score = float(trial_score)
                    accepted = True
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        improved_best = True

        if not accepted and since_any > 50:
            trial_p = None
            trial_score = float("inf")
            trial_c = None
            sigmas = (0.5 / float(n), 0.1 / float(n), 0.02 / float(n), 0.004 / float(n), 0.0008 / float(n))
            for sigma in sigmas:
                pnew = project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                cnew = conv_full(pnew)
                scnew = score_from_conv(n, cnew)
                if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < trial_score:
                    trial_score = scnew
                    trial_p = pnew
                    trial_c = cnew
            if trial_p is not None:
                temp = 0.0012 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(since_any) / 800.0)
                if should_accept_score(trial_score, cur_score, temp, rng):
                    prev_p = p.copy()
                    p = trial_p
                    c = trial_c
                    cur_score = float(trial_score)
                    accepted = True
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        improved_best = True

        if accepted:
            since_any = 0
        else:
            since_any += 1

        if improved_best:
            since_best = 0
        else:
            since_best += 1

        if not accepted and since_any > 400:
            mix = 0.7 if since_best < 600 else 0.4
            p = project_simplex(mix * best_p + (1.0 - mix) * p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3 / float(n), n))
            c = conv_full(p)
            cur_score = score_from_conv(n, c)
            prev_p = p.copy()
            since_any = 0
        elif since_best > 1200:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = conv_full(p)
            cur_score = float(best_score)
            prev_p = p.copy()
            since_any = 0
            since_best = 0

        if iter_no % 2000 == 0:
            print(label + " iter=%d best=%.10f cur=%.10f" % (iter_no, best_score, cur_score), flush=True)

        if best_score < 1.5000 and since_best > 8000:
            break

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_p)) or best_p.size == 0:
        best_p = p
    else:
        best_p = normalize(best_p)
    return best_p, float(best_score) if np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")


def optimize_candidate_poly(p0, rng, time_limit, allow_lp, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    sc = fast_score(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return p, float("inf")
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    use_lp = bool(allow_lp) and _HAVE_LP and n <= LP_MAX

    if use_lp and time_limit > 500.0:
        lp1 = min(240.0, max(30.0, time_limit * 0.22))
        loc1 = min(600.0, max(60.0, time_limit * 0.42))
        lp2 = min(150.0, max(20.0, time_limit * 0.15))
        loc2 = max(0.0, time_limit - lp1 - loc1 - lp2 - 20.0)

        if time.monotonic() < end - 30.0:
            rem = float(end - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
            p, sc = polish_lp(p, rng, min(lp1, rem), max_iters=6, label=label + " LP1 ")
        if time.monotonic() < end - 30.0:
            rem = float(end - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
            p, sc = local_optimize(p, rng, min(loc1, rem), label=label + " LOC1 ")
        if time.monotonic() < end - 30.0:
            rem = float(end - time.monotonic() - 30.0)
            p, sc = polish_lp(p, rng, min(lp2, rem), max_iters=5, label=label + " LP2 ")
        if time.monotonic() < end - 10.0:
            rem = float(end - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
            p, sc = local_optimize(p, rng, min(loc2, rem), label=label + " LOC2 ")
    else:
        p, sc = local_optimize(p, rng, time_limit, label=label)

    if use_lp and time.monotonic() < end - 30.0:
        rem = float(end - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
        if rem > 20.0:
            p, sc = polish_lp(p, rng, min(60.0, rem), max_iters=3, label=label + " LPf ")

    return p, float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else float("inf")


def score_list(seq):
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        return fast_score(arr)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2350.0
    total_budget = float(min(total_budget, 2350.0))
    if total_budget < 120.0:
        total_budget = 120.0
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 15.0

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    cands = []

    if parent is not None:
        p700 = resample_sequence(parent, 700)
        add_candidate(cands, p700, "parent700")
        add_candidate(cands, smooth_sequence(p700, 1.5), "parent700_sm1")
        add_candidate(cands, smooth_sequence(p700, 3.0), "parent700_sm2")
        add_candidate(cands, smooth_sequence(p700, 6.0), "parent700_sm3")

        if int(parent.size) != 700:
            add_candidate(cands, resample_sequence(parent, int(parent.size)), "parent_native")

        for m in (1000, 1400, 2048):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 10.0:
                break
            add_candidate(cands, resample_sequence(parent, m), "parent%d" % m)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        for p in make_parametric(700, rng):
            add_candidate(cands, p, "param700")

    if parent is None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60.0:
            for p in make_parametric(1400, rng, max_candidates=32):
                add_candidate(cands, p, "param1400")

    prune_candidates(cands, 14)

    if not cands:
        pu = make_uniform(700)
        add_candidate(cands, pu, "uniform700")

    cands.sort(key=cand_score_key)

    main_p = None
    main_label = None
    for sc, p, label in cands:
        if label == "parent700" or (parent is not None and int(p.size) == 700):
            main_p = p
            main_label = label
            break
    if main_p is None:
        for sc, p, label in cands:
            if int(p.size) <= LP_MAX:
                main_p = p
                main_label = label
                break
    if main_p is None:
        main_p = cands[0][1].copy()
        main_label = cands[0][2]

    aux = []
    for sc, p, label in cands:
        if label == main_label:
            continue
        if len(aux) >= 5:
            break
        aux.append((sc, p, label))

    final_list = []
    overall_p = main_p.copy()
    overall_score = fast_score(overall_p)
    final_list.append((float(overall_score), overall_p.copy(), "initial_main"))

    print("initial best internal=%.10f n=%d label=%s" % (overall_score, overall_p.size, main_label), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 240.0:
        main_time = min(1400.0, max(300.0, rem * 0.58))
        p, sc = optimize_candidate_poly(main_p, rng, main_time, True, "main ")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < overall_score:
            overall_p = p.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "main"))

    for sc0, p0, label in aux:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 180.0:
            break
        if int(p0.size) == int(overall_p.size):
            continue
        alloc = min(260.0, max(80.0, rem * 0.22))
        p, sc = optimize_candidate_poly(p0, rng, alloc, False, "aux ")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < overall_score:
            overall_p = p.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "aux_" + label))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 380.0:
        odd_budget = min(120.0, rem - 250.0)
        p_odd, sc_odd = search_odd_poly(700, rng, odd_budget)
        if p_odd is not None and np.isfinite(sc_odd):
            add_candidate(final_list, p_odd, "odd_raw")
            alloc = min(240.0, max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0))
            p, sc = optimize_candidate_poly(p_odd, rng, alloc, True, "odd ")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < overall_score:
                overall_p = p.copy()
                overall_score = float(sc)
                final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "odd"))

    if int(overall_p.size) == 700:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 180.0:
            p1400 = resample_sequence(overall_p, 1400)
            add_candidate(final_list, p1400, "ups700_1400")
            alloc = min(220.0, max(50.0, rem * 0.45))
            p, sc = optimize_candidate_poly(p1400, rng, alloc, False, "ups ")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < overall_score:
                overall_p = p.copy()
                overall_score = float(sc)
                final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "ups1400"))

    if int(overall_p.size) > 750:
        p_small = resample_sequence(overall_p, 700)
        sc_small = fast_score(p_small)
        if np.isfinite(sc_small) and sc_small < overall_score:
            overall_p = p_small.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc_small)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "downsample_main"))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        alloc = min(rem - 8.0, 650.0)
        p, sc = optimize_candidate_poly(overall_p, rng, alloc, True, "final ")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < overall_score:
            overall_p = p.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "final_polish"))

    for sigma in (1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
        p_s = smooth_sequence(overall_p, sigma)
        sc_s = fast_score(p_s)
        if np.isfinite(sc_s) and sc_s < overall_score:
            overall_p = p_s.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc_s)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "final_smooth_%s" % sigma))

    if overall_p.size > 700:
        p700 = resample_sequence(overall_p, 700)
        sc700 = fast_score(p700)
        if np.isfinite(sc700) and sc700 < overall_score:
            overall_p = p700.copy()
            overall_score = float(sc700)
            final_list.append((overall_score, overall_p.copy(), "final_downsample700"))

    add_candidate(final_list, overall_p, "best_internal")
    prune_candidates(final_list, 8)
    final_list.sort(key=cand_score_key)

    chosen_p = final_list[0][1]
    chosen_val = final_list[0][0]
    chosen_actual_key = float("inf")

    for sc, p, label in final_list[:8]:
        lst = [float(x) for x in p]
        actual = score_list(lst)
        if np.isfinite(actual):
            if actual < chosen_actual_key:
                chosen_actual_key = actual
                chosen_p = p
                chosen_val = actual

    chosen_p = normalize(chosen_p)
    out = [float(x) for x in chosen_p]

    actual = score_list(out)
    print("FINAL internal=%.10f actual=%.10f n=%d" % (chosen_val if np.isfinite(chosen_val) else 0.0, actual, len(out)), flush=True)

    return out
```
